# PUR-RETURN-01 采购退货与供应商结算调整

## 1. 业务目的、操作者和五条独立事实链

采购员从真实原收货行提出退货，保持原PO商业修订、ReceiptDomain、Root原坐标及批次谱系。供应商Owner签确切协议，审批Owner批准准确业务版本；采购授权并保护原范围，仓库人员在OUT完成封包、开始和实际退运，Finance人员按真实原义务作价值/应付调整。恢复人员只能沿原Part/Settlement工作查原结果，对账人员重建本域观察。 [原文 L14](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:14>)、[原文 L16](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:16>)

一个Case固定采购法人、供应商、商业原币及真实RMA身份，可跨PO/GR。一个ReturnLine固定原domain＋一个PO SourcePortion/源单位，可选多个不连续段；跨domain/portion必须分行，页面拆行不重生身份或预算。一个ExecutionPart是一行/同原Root的一次真实批次，可含多位置物理行，但只有一个原子Stock组；真正4＋6分批才建两个Part，未知10不能拆成新4/6重试。 [原文 L42](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:42>)、[原文 L461](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:461>)、[原文 L54068](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54068>)

允许拒收后退、接受后退、未验收但有准确Quality/Stock退运用途许可的退；第三路保持PENDING/UNKNOWN或acceptanceRef=null，不制造Accepted。普通可用量不能替代专用退运用途。补发/重购/客户退回/委外另有Owner；替换品只保存期待和真实新来源链接，不自动重开PO可收量、需求或采购单。 [原文 L44](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:44>)、[原文 L46](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:46>)、[原文 L176](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:176>)、[原文 L245](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:245>)、[原文 L258](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:258>)

商业DRAFT/FROZEN/APPROVAL_PENDING/AUTHORIZED/FENCED/CLOSED，物理NOT_STARTED/PREPARING/READY/PARTIAL/OUT_COMPLETE/UNKNOWN/CONFLICT，Finance NOT_ASSESSED/PLANNING/READY/PENDING/PARTIAL/ADJUSTED/NO_EFFECT_AUTHORIZED/UNKNOWN/CONFLICT，以及供应商/现金/抵扣分别显示。CLOSED仅不再接受新执行；当前完成须各轴有据且当前，不是永久保证。 [原文 L48](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:48>)、[原文 L713](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:713>)、[原文 L715](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:715>)

| RET-F08 selectedRanges | Range[]，Qty8字符串 | 原domain半开区间；正长度、升序、互不重叠；每行量是各段长之和，不手工填独立总量 | [原文 L86](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:86>) |
| RET-F09 unitMapping | RationalConversion＋ProofRef | 原BaseUom、PO SourceUom、供应商结算单位分别保全；无法精确表达返回PRECISION_BLOCKED | [原文 L87](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:87>) |
| RET-F10 physicalPurposeEvidence | ProofRef[] | Quality/Stock退运用途许可、当前货权/冻结/范围证明；UNKNOWN不能造Available=true | [原文 L88](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:88>) |
| RET-F11 agreement | SupplierAgreementRef | 精确签署版本、允许范围及结算方式；采购员不能填accepted=true模拟供应商Owner | [原文 L89](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:89>) |
| RET-F12 approval | ApprovalProof | 绑定完整BusinessDigest/SourceDigest/AgreementDigest；改变价量、范围、协议使旧决策不可用 | [原文 L90](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:90>) |
| RET-F13 requestedShipBy | DateText或null | 商业期望，不决定WMS真实发出时间；默认null | [原文 L91](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:91>) |
| RET-F14 destination | H31.LogisticsDestinationTarget.ref（EvidenceRef） | 供应商协议/主数据有权退运地址版本，外披另有权限；不得用自由地址绕开批准 | [原文 L92](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:92>) |
| RET-F15 executionParts | PartView[] | 返回真实part/allowance/OUT Work/当前状态；只在有权未执行范围创建part | [原文 L93](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:93>) |
| RET-F16 financialTerms | AgreementSettlementTerms | CASH_REFUND/AP_OFFSET/REPLACEMENT_EXPECTATION/NO_PAYMENT_EXPECTED等准确协议依据；没有协议不默认退款 | [原文 L94](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:94>) |
| RET-F17 amount/components | AmountComponent[] | 由Finance专业计划返回，Qty8/Amount8字符串，原币/角色/原Posting逐项；采购不能填首科目 | [原文 L95](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:95>) |
| RET-F18 statusWatermarks | OwnerHead[] | 每轴complete/current/asOf/coverage；无结果与完整0不同，历史成功独立保留 | [原文 L96](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:96>) |
| RET-F19 expectedVersion/commandId | Version/UUID | 页面GET返回版本及本次操作稳定命令键；传输attempt不换业务键 | [原文 L97](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:97>) |
| RET-F20 attachmentRefs/reason | ProofRef[]、string | 限Owner文件权限/类型/大小/审计；更改业务原件需真正有权后继，不仅替换附件名 | [原文 L98](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:98>) |

Id小写UUID；Key1–128；业务Seq从1，Counter/generation可0；Version是不透明CAS，不作算术。Ref三字段、EvidenceRef含digest、ProofRef含contractRef严格区分。Q8 decimal(21,8)最多13整数＋恰8小数；Amount/Price最多18整数＋8小数并受Finance原义务更严约束。单位比例用正互质有理数，数量不能round，PO Qty6桥必须完全准确；不同单位或币种不合总量。 [原文 L100](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:100>)、[原文 L132](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:132>)、[原文 L134](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:134>)、[原文 L136](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:136>)

### 公共值、草稿、页面和查询DTO

[原文 L142](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:142>)

```ts
type Id=string; type Key=string; type Seq=string; type Counter=string; type Version=string;
type Q8=string; type Amount8=string; type Price8=string; type Digest=string; type At=string; type DateText=string;
type Mode='REAL'|'DEMO'; type Coverage='COMPLETE'|'PARTIAL'|'UNKNOWN'|'CONFLICT';
interface Ref { owner:Key; id:Key; version:Key; }
interface EvidenceRef { owner:Key; id:Key; version:Key; digest:Digest; }
interface ProofRef { ref:Ref; digest:Digest; contractRef:Ref; }
interface UnitRef { id:Key; version:Key; }
interface Range { from:Q8; to:Q8; }
interface RootSpan { rootId:Id; from:Q8; to:Q8; }
interface Ratio { numerator:string; denominator:string; fromUom:UnitRef; toUom:UnitRef; proof:ProofRef; }
interface Gate { code:Key; allowed:boolean; evidence:ProofRef[]; missing:Key[]; }
interface Problem { status:number; code:Key; message:string; commandId:Id|null; currentVersion:Version|null; retry:'NONE'|'SAME_KEY'|'QUERY_ORIGINAL'|'NEW_INTENT_AFTER_REVIEW'; errors:{field:string;code:Key}[]; traceId:Key; }
interface CommandResult { commandId:Id; state:'SUCCEEDED'|'ACCEPTED'|'REJECTED'|'SEALED_NO_EFFECT'; replayed:boolean; returnId:Id|null; version:Version|null; resourceRef:Ref|null; workId:Id|null; errorCode:Key|null; pollUrl:string|null; }
interface OwnerHead { owner:Key; streamKey:Key; observedHigh:Counter; appliedSequence:Counter; currentKnowledge:'CURRENT_AT_AS_OF'|'NEEDS_HEAD_CHECK'|'GAP'|'UNKNOWN'|'CONFLICT'; headProof:ProofRef|null; completeManifestDigest:Digest|null; asOf:At|null; }
interface OriginIdentity { owner:Key; kind:Key; key:Key; proof:ProofRef; }
type ReturnReason='QUALITY_REJECTED'|'POST_ACCEPTANCE'|'UNACCEPTED_AUTHORIZED';
interface DraftLineInput { lineId:Id|null; lineNumber:number; receiptOperationId:Id; receiptLineId:Id; sourcePortionId:Id; expectedDomainRef:EvidenceRef; ranges:Range[]; reason:ReturnReason; reasonText:string; }
interface DraftInput { logisticsTargetRef:EvidenceRef; buyerEntityId:Id; supplierId:Key; currency:Key; originIdentity:OriginIdentity; supplierReturnNo:string|null; originalDocument:ProofRef; requestedShipBy:DateText|null; requestedResolution:'AP_OFFSET'|'CASH_REFUND'|'REPLACEMENT_EXPECTATION'|'UNDECIDED'; lines:DraftLineInput[]; notes:string|null; attachmentRefs:ProofRef[]; }
interface SavedLine extends Omit<DraftLineInput,'lineId'> { lineId:Id; }
interface SavedDraft extends Omit<DraftInput,'lines'> { lines:SavedLine[]; }
interface SaveDraft { expectedVersion:Version; draft:DraftInput; }
interface ReturnView { returnId:Id; displayNumber:Key; identityKey:Key; version:Version; businessRevision:Seq; draft:SavedDraft; frozenReturnRef:Ref|null; permitRef:EvidenceRef|null; commercialState:'DRAFT'|'FROZEN'|'APPROVAL_PENDING'|'AUTHORIZED'|'FENCED'|'CLOSED'; physicalState:'NOT_STARTED'|'PREPARING'|'READY'|'PARTIAL'|'OUT_COMPLETE'|'UNKNOWN'|'CONFLICT'; financeState:'NOT_ASSESSED'|'PLANNING'|'READY'|'PENDING'|'PARTIAL'|'ADJUSTED'|'NO_EFFECT_AUTHORIZED'|'UNKNOWN'|'CONFLICT'; resolutionState:'UNDECIDED'|'PENDING'|'SATISFIED'|'DISPUTED'|'NOT_APPLICABLE'; currentCompletion:boolean; verifiedAsOf:At|null; gates:Gate[]; mode:Mode; }
interface ReturnSearch { buyerEntityId:Id; supplierId:Key|null; number:string|null; poId:Id|null; receiptLineId:Id|null; commercialState:ReturnView['commercialState']|null; physicalState:ReturnView['physicalState']|null; financeState:ReturnView['financeState']|null; dateFrom:DateText|null; dateTo:DateText|null; cursor:Key|null; snapshotToken:Key|null; pageSize:20|50|100; sort:'UPDATED_DESC'|'NUMBER_ASC'; }
interface ReturnPage { items:ReturnView[]; nextCursor:Key|null; snapshotToken:Key; coverage:Coverage; asOf:At; }
interface VersionedReason { expectedVersion:Version; reason:string; }
```

IdentityKey由environment/tenant/buyerEntity/supplier/originOwner/kind/key决定，不含ReturnVersion或显示号；原件后继保原身份，不能改Qty再注册同一RMA。BusinessDigest签SavedDraft完整业务字段，lineId/range/attachment按具名键排序；用户requestedResolution只是期望，不等正式协议。 [原文 L170](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:170>)、[原文 L172](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:172>)

### 完整原domain、映射、用途位置、SourceBasis与预览

[原文 L178](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:178>)

```ts
interface OriginalDomain { domainKey:Key; domainRef:EvidenceRef; receiptOperationId:Id; receiptLineId:Id; physicalReceiptRef:Ref; inboundReceiptLineRef:Ref; receiptSource:{producer:Key;sourceKind:Key;documentId:Key;lineId:Key;executionKey:Key;sourceVersion:Key}; partKey:Key; rootId:Id; originalMovementId:Id; originalStockReceiptRef:Ref; originalRange:Range; baseUom:UnitRef; dimensionsProof:ProofRef; }
interface SourceMapRow { mapKey:Key; domainRanges:Range[]; rootSpans:RootSpan[]; receiptSliceRef:Ref; sourcePortionId:Id; sourceUom:UnitRef; baseToSource:Ratio; originalPoCommercial:ProofRef; originalPoUnitPrice:Price8; priceUom:UnitRef; baseToPrice:Ratio; }
interface QualityPartition { ranges:Range[]; state:'ACCEPTED'|'REJECTED'|'HOLD'|'PENDING'|'UNKNOWN'; decisionProof:ProofRef|null; applicationProof:ProofRef|null; }
interface ReturnPurposeProof { purposeRef:UnitRef; ranges:Range[]; qualityAuthorization:ProofRef|null; stockPurposeAuthorization:ProofRef; technicalAuthorization:ProofRef|null; current:true; validUntil:At; }
interface PhysicalPosition { positionKey:Key; poolId:Id; poolRevision:Counter; rootSpan:RootSpan; place:STK.Place; dimensions:STK.StockDimensions; purposePermission:ProofRef; claimCoverage:'UNCLAIMED'|'CLAIMED'|'UNKNOWN'; }
interface SourceBasis { basisId:Id; selectedSourcePortionId:Id; returnLineId:Id; origin:OriginalDomain; buyerEntityId:Id; supplierId:Key; siteId:Id; itemRef:EvidenceRef; poId:Id; poRevisionRef:Ref; poLineId:Id; scheduleId:Id; currency:Key; commercialReceiptRef:Ref; scopeSetRef:EvidenceRef; effectiveScopeRef:EvidenceRef; basisRevision:Seq; effectiveRanges:Range[]; excludedOriginalRanges:Range[]; acceptanceRef:Ref|null; quality:QualityPartition[]; mapRows:SourceMapRow[]; purposeProofs:ReturnPurposeProof[]; physicalPositions:PhysicalPosition[]; currentOwnerHeads:OwnerHead[]; coverage:Coverage; current:boolean; validUntil:At; }
interface SourceCandidateQuery { returnId:Id; receiptOperationId:Id|null; receiptLineId:Id|null; poId:Id|null; cursor:Key|null; }
interface SourceCandidate { candidateKey:Key; receiptOperationId:Id; receiptLineId:Id; sourcePortionId:Id; sourceUom:UnitRef; originalDomainRef:EvidenceRef; displayBaseQuantity:Q8|null; baseUom:UnitRef; qualitySummary:Key; physicalReturnPurposeStatus:'ELIGIBLE'|'BLOCKED'|'UNKNOWN'; selectable:boolean; reasons:Key[]; }
interface SourceCandidatePage { items:SourceCandidate[]; nextCursor:Key|null; coverage:Coverage; queryProof:ProofRef; }
interface ResolveSources { expectedVersion:Version; receiptLineIds:Id[]; }
interface SourceResolution { returnId:Id; returnVersion:Version; bases:SourceBasis[]; missing:Key[]; }
interface PreviewRequest { expectedVersion:Version; basisIds:Id[]; evaluationAt:At; }
interface PreviewLine { returnLineId:Id; basisId:Id; domainKey:Key; ranges:Range[]; baseQuantity:Q8; baseUom:UnitRef; sourceQuantities:{sourcePortionId:Id;quantity:string;uom:UnitRef;receiptSliceRef:Ref}[]; reason:ReturnReason; sourceCurrent:boolean; gates:Gate[]; }
interface Preview { previewId:Id; returnId:Id; version:Version; businessDigest:Digest; sourceSetDigest:Digest; lines:PreviewLine[]; requiredPolicyRefs:ProofRef[]; canFreeze:boolean; checkedAt:At; expiresAt:At; }
interface Freeze { expectedVersion:Version; previewId:Id; expectedBusinessDigest:Digest; expectedSourceSetDigest:Digest; reason:string; }
interface ReturnVersion { ref:Ref; returnId:Id; businessRevision:Seq; previousRef:Ref|null; businessDigest:Digest; sourceSetDigest:Digest; draft:SavedDraft; bases:SourceBasis[]; selectedLines:PreviewLine[]; createdAt:At; }
```

DomainKey只绑定真实原domain owner/id及环境租户法人，scope/slice/位置/版本不重生预算。Root/原区间/单位一旦改变属于身份冲突。每MapRow逐段映原Root Span、ReceiptSlice和PO portion，不凭总量等同；physicalPositions只是快照，最终仍查真正当前位置/Claim/用途。SourceSetDigest只排SourceBasis顶层validUntil及currentOwnerHeads.asOf，不能递归删所有同名字段，嵌套用途有效期/原head proof保留。 [原文 L197](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:197>)、[原文 L199](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:199>)、[原文 L201](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:201>)

```ts
interface AgreementScope { returnLineId:Id; domainKey:Key; ranges:Range[]; baseUom:UnitRef; }
interface SettlementTerms { method:'AP_OFFSET'|'CASH_REFUND'|'REPLACEMENT_EXPECTATION'|'NO_PAYMENT_EXPECTED'; currency:Key; professionalTermsRef:ProofRef; destinationRef:ProofRef; replacementExpectationRef:ProofRef|null; }
interface SupplierAgreement { logisticsTargetRef:EvidenceRef; ref:ProofRef; returnId:Id; supplierId:Key; status:'ACCEPTED'|'REJECTED'|'WITHDRAWN'|'UNKNOWN'; previousRef:Ref|null; scopeDigest:Digest; scopes:AgreementScope[]; terms:SettlementTerms; signedBy:Key; effectiveAt:At; expiresAt:At|null; currentHead:OwnerHead; }
interface AdoptAgreement { expectedVersion:Version; agreementRef:ProofRef; reason:string; }
interface ApprovalSubject { logisticsTargetRef:EvidenceRef; returnVersionRef:Ref; businessDigest:Digest; sourceSetDigest:Digest; agreementRef:ProofRef; agreementScopeDigest:Digest; scopes:AgreementScope[]; ruleRef:ProofRef; }
interface ApprovalRequest { expectedVersion:Version; subject:ApprovalSubject; subjectDigest:Digest; reason:string; }
interface ApprovalDecision { ref:ProofRef; subjectDigest:Digest; decision:'APPROVED'|'REJECTED'|'REVOKED'; approver:Key; ruleRef:ProofRef; scopes:AgreementScope[]; currentHead:OwnerHead; effectiveAt:At; expiresAt:At|null; }
interface AuthorizeRequest { expectedVersion:Version; returnVersionRef:Ref; agreementRef:ProofRef; approvalRef:ProofRef; reason:string; }
interface ProtectedRange { protectionId:Id; returnId:Id; returnVersionRef:Ref; returnLineId:Id; domainKey:Key; ranges:Range[]; baseUom:UnitRef; state:'AUTHORIZED'|'PART_RESERVED'|'PART_UNKNOWN'|'PHYSICALLY_RETURNED'|'RELEASED_UNUSED'|'RETIRED'|'SUCCESSOR_HELD'|'SUPERSEDED_HELD'|'PARTITIONED'; sourceProofs:ProofRef[]; }
interface ParentPermitView { parentPermitRef:EvidenceRef; authorizationProof:ProofRef; ownerBindings:ProofRef[]; returnVersionRef:Ref; authorizationDigest:Digest; controlRevision:Counter; epoch:Seq; state:'ENABLED'|'FENCED'|'CLOSED'; scopes:AgreementScope[]; approvalRef:ProofRef; agreementRef:ProofRef; protections:ProtectedRange[]; reverseControlRef:ProofRef; gates:Gate[]; }
interface PlanPart { expectedVersion:Version; parentPermitRef:EvidenceRef; returnLineId:Id; ranges:Range[]; reason:string; }
interface PartView { partId:Id; returnId:Id; returnLineId:Id; executionPartKey:Key; version:Version; parentPermitRef:EvidenceRef; partPermitRef:EvidenceRef; permitBusinessDigest:Digest|null; authorizationRef:EvidenceRef|null; requestKey:Id; requestDigest:Digest|null; state:'PLANNED'|'PREPARING'|'READY'|'STARTED'|'OUT_COMPLETE'|'FENCING'|'SEALED_NO_EFFECT'|'REJECTED_NO_EFFECT'|'UNKNOWN'|'RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED'; plannedRanges:Range[]; actualRanges:Range[]; unresolvedRanges:Range[]; outWorkRef:EvidenceRef|null; dispatchResultRef:EvidenceRef|null; closureRef:EvidenceRef|null; gates:Gate[]; }
interface PartCommand { expectedVersion:Version; partId:Id; reason:string; }
interface PartPage { items:PartView[]; nextCursor:Key|null; coverage:Coverage; }
```

### 紧凑投影与完整有权原文的绑定

[原文 L232](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:232>)

```ts
interface ProjectionBinding { bindingRef:ProofRef; kind:'SUPPLIER_AGREEMENT'|'APPROVAL_DECISION'|'PARENT_AUTHORIZATION'|'MATCH_RETURN_GUARD'; projectionRef:EvidenceRef; completeProof:ProofRef; returnVersionRef:Ref; returnLineId:Id; originalDomainRef:EvidenceRef; exactRanges:Range[]; baseUom:UnitRef; projectionSchemaRef:Ref; completeSchemaRef:Ref; fieldMappingRef:ProofRef; fullBusinessDigest:Digest; fullSourceSetDigest:Digest; approvalSubjectDigest:Digest; purposeRef:UnitRef; agreementProof:ProofRef; approvalProof:ProofRef; currentSourceHead:OwnerHead; valid:boolean; mode:Mode; }
interface ParentAuthorizationBody { logisticsTargetRef:EvidenceRef; returnVersionRef:Ref; businessDigest:Digest; sourceSetDigest:Digest; approvalRef:ProofRef; agreementRef:ProofRef; scopes:AgreementScope[]; reverseControlRef:ProofRef; }
```

compact审批/协议/父许可/MRX envelope的hash只证明自己的短body，不能冒完整业务摘要。ProjectionBinding先于Seed/Authorization保存，双向指准确已存在投影与完整proof，projection owner/id/version唯一绑定；完整协议、subject、物流target、用途、源范围/单位/当前head均须有权Producer真实解析。缺绑定或暗换完整原件阻Prepare/START/EXECUTE，OUT只信SourceParticipant准确结论。 [原文 L230](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:230>)、[原文 L237](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:237>)、[原文 L239](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:239>)、[原文 L241](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:241>)、[原文 L956](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:956>)

拟议schema pur_return：所有FK含Tenant/Environment；Q21,8、A26,8、Counter bigint，typed JSON有封闭DTO及检索列。RETURN不建影子Finance义务账，MATCH表归MATCH；当前头与原immutable业务原文分开。区间相交必须同Domain锁，UQ(from,to)不能防0–10与5–15。 [原文 L873](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:873>)、[原文 L929](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:929>)、[原文 L933](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:933>)

## 4. 登记、授权、分批退运、修订与结算主流程

### 4.1 来源和准确授权

登记先有权真实身份/主数据，IdentitySlot内同义回原Case、异义409；草稿每次新Revision保旧行。resolve/preview只读当前原GR/PO/Quality/Stock用途，无Claim/Allowance/保护效果；GR handoff因未完整验收不能200时，专门只读适配核原domain、scope与真实用途，不伪GR200或PASS。Freeze当前源+Case CAS追加不可变版本，尚无批准/协议时不取执行额度。 [原文 L245](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:245>)、[原文 L254](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:254>)、[原文 L256](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:256>)、[原文 L258](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:258>)、[原文 L838](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:838>)、[原文 L840](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:840>)

物流target先由有权Owner保存supplier/consignee/addressVersion完整目标，再由协议和ApprovalSubject绑定target，之后产生AuthorizedDestination；金融抵扣账户不是物流地址。允许准确批准第三方收货人，不泛化任何供应商地址。最终custody proof还须同Part/Root范围/target，地址或收货人变更需受控后继新批准。 [原文 L323](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:323>)、[原文 L330](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:330>)、[原文 L332](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:332>)、[原文 L334](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:334>)、[原文 L54324](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54324>)、[原文 L54326](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54326>)、[原文 L54328](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54328>)、[原文 L54330](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54330>)

AuthorizationPreparation先固定MRX controlKey并安装反向控制；未知查原键，安装成功但本域失败保Preparation/overlay可恢复。最终共同Uow重核协议、审批subject、源用途、所有Domain/MRX当前控制，全范围保护＋ParentPermit＋Generation＋Audit一次commit，任一重叠整申请失败。授权前取消先封Preparation所有未来路径，晚到install也核这个门，不容匿名永久overlay或自行删保护。 [原文 L222](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:222>)、[原文 L224](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:224>)、[原文 L842](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:842>)、[原文 L844](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:844>)

### 4.2 一个真实Part的生成和物理提交

固定生成顺序：版本/完整批准协议→MRX→父授权/保护→compact投影及绑定→真实Part/独立PartPermit/DispatchDefinition→OriginMapping→Seed→逐child真实Allowance注册回执→Manifest/PostingTemplate→permitBusinessDigest→ReturnAuthorization→动态Guard/资源计划→实际结果。前一步不能引用后一步未来hash；父authorizationDigest与单Part permitBusinessDigest不是同一语义。 [原文 L453](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:453>)、[原文 L455](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:455>)、[原文 L457](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:457>)、[原文 L500](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:500>)、[原文 L54255](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54255>)

PlanPart在同原域/current父许可/MRX锁中从未用范围建立PartReserved；位置按准确原Root Span取得，不能FIFO换同Lot别Root。首批AUTHORIZED_FREE且claimSpends=[]，相交Claim先RSV有权处置，采购不能UNRSV或清Hold。每child Grant三Source身份必须等该child，quantity仅其子量且不交叠全覆盖Part；注册未知只查原allowanceKey，不能换SourcePartKey重领。 [原文 L461](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:461>)、[原文 L463](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:463>)、[原文 L465](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:465>)、[原文 L467](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:467>)、[原文 L469](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:469>)、[原文 L471](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:471>)

Manifest与PostingTemplate逐行双向匹配child六字段、Occurrence、基本/输入单位量、allowanceRef；SHIP只能IssueLine，不用入向Stock RETURN。Prepare前本地已存requestKey/digest；READY只表示计划齐，实退仍0。完整封包后START与SourceStartReceipt同Uow，物理仍0。Execute先查同原Part成功结果，新的效果再逐项核当前资源、批准/协议/绑定、用途、MRX、Source/Stock原计划。 [原文 L473](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:473>)、[原文 L504](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:504>)、[原文 L506](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:506>)、[原文 L508](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:508>)、[原文 L510](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:510>)

唯一实际写序是Stock→OUT暂存DispatchPhysicalFact→RETURN实际范围＋MRX PhysicalApply→SourceReceipt→OUT DispatchResult/SourceMap/Package SHIPPED/Outbox，顶层一次commit。物理事实不引用未来SourceReceipt/Result，MRX从同Uow reader核完整Stock真结果和原映射；内部COMMITTED候选不能提前外发。任一步失败包括MRX全回滚；外来已有部分效果只RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED，不跳过成功行补做原组剩量。 [原文 L512](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:512>)、[原文 L514](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:514>)、[原文 L54334](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54334>)、[原文 L54337](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54337>)、[原文 L54338](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54338>)、[原文 L54339](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54339>)、[原文 L54340](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54340>)、[原文 L54341](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54341>)、[原文 L54342](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54342>)、[原文 L54344](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54344>)

```ts
interface BeginRevision { expectedVersion:Version; predecessorRef:Ref; requestedScopes:AgreementScope[]; reason:string; }
interface RevisionSourceDisposition { ref:ProofRef; returnId:Id; predecessorRef:Ref; predecessorPermitRef:EvidenceRef|null; predecessorFenceRef:ProofRef; sourceSlotGeneration:Counter; completeSlotManifest:ProofRef; partClosureRefs:EvidenceRef[]; retainedActualScopes:AgreementScope[]; heldUnusedScopes:AgreementScope[]; unresolvedScopes:AgreementScope[]; futurePredecessorExecutionBlocked:true; }
interface RevisionView { revisionId:Id; returnId:Id; predecessorRef:Ref; version:Version; state:'FENCING'|'WAITING_CLOSURES'|'DRAFT'|'FROZEN'|'APPROVAL_PENDING'|'AUTHORIZED'|'ABANDONED'|'UNKNOWN'|'CONFLICT'; requestedScopes:AgreementScope[]; disposition:RevisionSourceDisposition|null; draft:SavedDraft|null; preview:Preview|null; successorRef:Ref|null; successorPermitRef:EvidenceRef|null; transferReceipt:ProofRef|null; errors:Key[]; }
interface SaveRevisionDraft { expectedVersion:Version; draft:DraftInput; reason:string; }
interface FreezeRevision { expectedVersion:Version; previewId:Id; expectedBusinessDigest:Digest; expectedSourceSetDigest:Digest; reason:string; }
interface AuthorizeRevision { expectedVersion:Version; successorRef:Ref; agreementRef:ProofRef; approvalRef:ProofRef; expectedPredecessorControlRef:ProofRef|null; reason:string; }
interface AbandonRevision { expectedVersion:Version; reason:string; }
```

| GET /revisions/{revisionId} | 无body→RevisionView；read | 查询原 Work，不新建后继 | [原文 L281](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:281>) |
| POST /revisions/{revisionId}/recover | VersionedReason→RevisionView；recover | 驱动原槽 fence/查询/关闭；无新效果 | [原文 L282](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:282>) |
| PUT /revisions/{revisionId}/draft | SaveRevisionDraft→RevisionView；edit | 仅该 Revision DRAFT；draft 固定 Case 身份和原行，ranges 必须等 requestedScopes；改物流目标/条款需新批准 | [原文 L283](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:283>) |
| POST /revisions/{revisionId}/resolve-sources | ResolveSources→SourceResolution；source.read | expectedVersion核Revision CAS；从该修订Draft读取准确原行，返回绑定该Revision的新BasisId，不复用旧Case草稿Basis | [原文 L284](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:284>) |
| POST /revisions/{revisionId}/preview | PreviewRequest→RevisionView；source.read | expectedVersion 为 Revision CAS；preview 绑定新草稿和当前源，旧已退范围仅历史 | [原文 L285](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:285>) |

非CLOSED Case仅一个活动RevisionWork；T12永久fence前驱父及全部新Part/别名/旧lease、捕获全SourceSlot generation，区别于CaseClose封全生命期。T13全槽逐一核：实际4保原版本，可能发出的6未知则draft=null；完整分割actual/heldUnused且无unresolved后，requested只可非空heldUnused子集，不能原10增12。旧MRX仍持有，放弃修订也不自然返全域。 [原文 L266](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:266>)、[原文 L291](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:291>)、[原文 L293](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:293>)、[原文 L297](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:297>)

T14用MRX.TransferPreparation无效果准备，同MainUow锁旧/新controls、Domain/Case/Revision与当前批准，精确pending范围转新control，旧TRANSFERRED_HELD/新Control/真实Receipt＋RETURN SUPERSEDED_HELD/新叶/完整TRANSFER边＋Case指针一次commit；旧实际不转归新版本。无前驱MRX须全no-install证明才同点直装新控制，不假转交收据。 [原文 L295](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:295>)、[原文 L310](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:310>)、[原文 L319](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:319>)、[原文 L336](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:336>)

### 完整保护继承图与关闭收据

[原文 L342](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:342>)

```ts
interface ProtectionInheritanceNode { protectionId:Id; returnId:Id; returnVersionRef:Ref; returnLineId:Id; domainKey:Key; ranges:Range[]; baseUom:UnitRef; state:ProtectedRange['state']; stateRevision:Counter; stateProof:ProofRef; }
interface ProtectionInheritanceEdge { edgeRef:ProofRef; kind:'TRANSFER'|'PARTITION'; predecessorProtectionId:Id; successorProtectionIds:Id[]; exactRanges:Range[]; domainGeneration:Counter; transferReceiptRef:ProofRef|null; effectOrClosureRef:ProofRef; }
interface ProtectionLineageSnapshot { ref:ProofRef; domainKey:Key; domainGeneration:Counter; rootProtectionIds:Id[]; nodes:ProtectionInheritanceNode[]; edges:ProtectionInheritanceEdge[]; activeTerminalRanges:Range[]; retainedActualRanges:Range[]; releasedTerminalRanges:Range[]; unresolvedRanges:Range[]; currentProtectedRanges:Range[]; verifiedAsOf:At; coverage:'COMPLETE'|'UNKNOWN'|'CONFLICT'; current:boolean; }
interface ProtectionLineageRead { domainKey:Key; rootProtectionIds:Id[]; expectedDomainGeneration:Counter; }
interface ProtectionLineageDecision { request:ProtectionLineageRead; snapshot:ProtectionLineageSnapshot|null; allowed:boolean; errorCodes:Key[]; quantityEffect:false; }
interface ProtectionClosureCommit { ref:ProofRef; returnId:Id; closedReturnVersionRef:Ref; previousDomainGeneration:Counter; domainGeneration:Counter; parentFenceRef:ProofRef; sourceCloseSummary:MRX.SourceCloseSummary; mrxControlAfter:MRX.Control; changedTerminalNodes:ProtectionInheritanceNode[]; completeLineageRef:ProofRef; allAffectedRoots:Id[]; futureExecutionBlocked:true; }
```

CurrentProtectedUnion=activeTerminal∪retainedActual∪unresolved；历史SUPERSEDED_HELD/PARTITIONED必须解析完整真正后继才递归计算。所有Case根全集受RootRegistry predicate门，不按当前Case版本过滤；每边子段完整无重叠、并集等祖先、唯一父、无环、generation递增。缺后继或原件保持对应祖先为unresolved，不能简单排除历史行。实际退叶永远不因普通关闭或credit释放；准确unused叶全路径永久fence才贡献0。 [原文 L340](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:340>)、[原文 L351](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:351>)、[原文 L355](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:355>)、[原文 L356](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:356>)、[原文 L357](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:357>)、[原文 L358](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:358>)、[原文 L359](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:359>)

T06实际2将原6分为已退2/持有4，Stock事实、MRX和保护边同commit；T08只关闭真正未用叶，同Domain/Lineage generation一次保存MRX后继、叶state、ClosureCommit/Audit。旧4＋后继6全关闭只保护4；后继2实际再关4则保护6；后续财务待办不使已准确释放的unused复占。关闭/新Part/再次转交竞争用同CaseLifetimeFence与slot/domain generation，旧关闭不能跳过新叶。 [原文 L361](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:361>)、[原文 L363](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:363>)、[原文 L365](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:365>)、[原文 L367](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:367>)

### 4.4 结算请求与专业原义务防重

### Finance原义务、计划、控制、结果与关闭完整类型

[原文 L644](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:644>)

```ts
interface ObligationScope { domainKey:Key; originalDomainRef:EvidenceRef; ranges:Range[]; baseUom:UnitRef; poId:Id; poLineId:Id; sourcePortionId:Id; receiptSliceRef:Ref; }
interface FinancialScope extends ObligationScope { returnLineId:Id; baseQuantity:Q8; dispatchResultRef:EvidenceRef; stockMovementRef:Ref; }
interface Money { role:'NET'|'TAX'|'FEE'|'GROSS'|'VALUE'; amount:Amount8; currency:Key; originalAmountRef:ProofRef; }
interface FinanceBasisRequest { settlementKey:Key; returnVersionRef:Ref; agreementRef:ProofRef; scopes:FinancialScope[]; knownMatchRefs:Ref[]; mode:Mode; }
interface OriginalObligation { obligationKey:Key; bookId:Key; kind:'GRNI'|'AP_ALLOCATION'|'INVENTORY_VALUE'|'PAYMENT_ALLOCATION'; originalFact:ProofRef; originalPostingRefs:ProofRef[]; scope:ObligationScope; originalComponents:Money[]; currentComponents:Money[]; currentHead:OwnerHead; coverage:Coverage; }
interface PriorAdjustment { canonicalAdjustmentKey:Key; causeRefs:ProofRef[]; originalObligationKey:Key; scope:ObligationScope; components:Money[]; state:'IN_FLIGHT'|'POSTED'|'REVERSED'|'UNKNOWN'; effectRefs:ProofRef[]; currentHead:OwnerHead; }
interface ForwardAPExposure { originalMatchRef:Ref; originalRequestKey:Key; originalAdmissionRef:ProofRef; originalPlanRef:ProofRef|null; exactScope:ObligationScope; state:'DRAFT_NO_EXECUTE'|'MAY_HAVE_BEEN_SENT'|'PARTIAL_EFFECT'|'CLOSED_WITH_PROOF'; allPathClosureRef:ProofRef|null; professionalDispositionRef:ProofRef|null; currentHead:OwnerHead; }
interface FinanceBasis { basisRef:ProofRef; settlementKey:Key; sourceState:'UNVALUED'|'GRNI_ONLY'|'AP_DRAFT'|'AP_PENDING'|'AP_POSTED'|'PARTIALLY_POSTED'|'PAID'|'MIXED'|'UNKNOWN'; obligations:OriginalObligation[]; priorAdjustments:PriorAdjustment[]; pendingForwardAPs:ForwardAPExposure[]; professionalPolicy:ProofRef; scopeManifest:ProofRef; currentHead:OwnerHead; coverage:Coverage; }
interface FinancePreparationInput { settlementKey:Key; returnId:Id; returnVersionRef:Ref; buyerEntityId:Id; supplierId:Key; currency:Key; agreementRef:ProofRef; terms:SettlementTerms; scopes:FinancialScope[]; financeBasisRef:ProofRef; basisDigest:Digest; mode:Mode; }
interface AdjustmentCoverage { claimKey:Key; originalObligationKey:Key; canonicalAdjustmentKey:Key; scope:FinancialScope; action:'CREATE'|'REUSE_EXISTING'|'WAIT_EXISTING'|'NO_EFFECT_REQUIRED'; expectedObligationRevision:Seq; originalComponents:Money[]; remainingAuthorizedComponents:Money[]; requestedComponents:Money[]; priorEffectRefs:ProofRef[]; authority:ProofRef; }
interface FinanceLegPlan { legKey:Key; bookId:Key; effect:'INVENTORY_VALUE_ADJUSTMENT'|'GRNI_ADJUSTMENT'|'AP_CREDIT'|'AP_OFFSET'; direction:'INCREASE'|'DECREASE'; adjustmentClaimKeys:Key[]; components:Money[]; policyRef:ProofRef; professionalApprovalRef:ProofRef; required:boolean; }
interface FinanceNoEffectProof { ref:ProofRef; settlementKey:Key; inputDigest:Digest; exactScopes:FinancialScope[]; scopeManifest:ProofRef; checkedObligationHeads:OwnerHead[]; reason:'NO_RECOGNIZED_OBLIGATION'|'ALREADY_SATISFIED_BY_EXACT_EFFECTS'; priorEffectRefs:ProofRef[]; policyRef:ProofRef; authority:ProofRef; complete:true; }
interface FinancePlan { planRef:ProofRef; settlementKey:Key; inputDigest:Digest; version:Seq; previousPlanRef:Ref|null; purpose:'PROCUREMENT_RETURN_ADJUSTMENT'; claims:AdjustmentCoverage[]; requiredLegs:FinanceLegPlan[]; noEffectProof:FinanceNoEffectProof|null; currentFinanceHead:OwnerHead; allObligationsCovered:boolean; coverage:Coverage; canExecute:boolean; expiresAt:At; gates:Gate[]; }
interface SettlementControl { controlRef:ProofRef; settlementKey:Key; inputDigest:Digest; planRef:ProofRef; epoch:Seq; controlRevision:Counter; state:'ENABLED'|'FENCED'; returnVersionRef:Ref; exactScopeDigest:Digest; agreementRef:ProofRef; financeAuthoritySetDigest:Digest; }
interface FinanceExecute { input:FinancePreparationInput; plan:FinancePlan; control:SettlementControl; }
interface FinanceLegResult { legKey:Key; bookId:Key; state:'PENDING'|'CREATED'|'POSTED'|'FAILED'|'REVERSED'|'UNKNOWN'; originalObligationKeys:Key[]; canonicalAdjustmentKeys:Key[]; effectRefs:ProofRef[]; components:Money[]; errorCodes:Key[]; }
interface FinanceResult { resultRef:ProofRef; settlementKey:Key; inputDigest:Digest; planRef:ProofRef; controlEpoch:Seq; scopeDigest:Digest; revision:Seq; previousRevision:Seq|null; scopeManifest:ProofRef; legs:FinanceLegResult[]; noEffectProof:FinanceNoEffectProof|null; professionalDispositionRef:ProofRef|null; outcome:'RECEIVED'|'PARTIAL'|'APPLIED'|'NO_EFFECT_AUTHORIZED'|'REJECTED'|'UNKNOWN'; receipt:ProofRef; occurredAt:At; }
interface FinanceQuery { settlementKey:Key; inputDigest:Digest; }
type FinanceQueryResult={status:'FOUND';result:FinanceResult;currentHead:OwnerHead;canInferNoEffect:false}|{status:'NOT_OBSERVED'|'UNAVAILABLE';settlementKey:Key;inputDigest:Digest;canInferNoEffect:false};
interface SettlementLocalClosure { ref:ProofRef; settlementKey:Key; inputDigest:Digest|null; parentWorkId:Id; controlEpoch:Seq; fencedThroughRevision:Counter; executedCallCount:0; allExecutePathsCovered:true; futureExecutionBlocked:true; prepareContractNoEffect:ProofRef; externalEffectsKnown:false; }
interface CancelSettlement { expectedVersion:Version; settlementId:Id; reason:string; }
interface FinanceClose { fenceRecordRef:ProofRef; closeKey:Id; settlementKey:Key; inputDigest:Digest; planRef:ProofRef; controlEpoch:Seq; expectedControlRevision:Counter; reason:string; }
interface FinanceClosure { fenceRecordRef:ProofRef; coveredExecutionEpochs:Seq[]; closureRef:ProofRef; closeKey:Id; settlementKey:Key; inputDigest:Digest; planRef:ProofRef; controlEpoch:Seq; fencedThroughRevision:Counter; coveredLegKeys:Key[]; uncoveredLegKeys:Key[]; outstandingEffectRefs:ProofRef[]; retainedHistoricalEffectRefs:ProofRef[]; futureEffectsClosed:boolean; result:'NO_EFFECT_ALL_PATHS'|'EFFECT_EXISTS'|'UNKNOWN'; currentHead:OwnerHead; }
interface PrepareSettlement { expectedVersion:Version; scopeLineIds:Id[]; agreementRef:ProofRef; reason:string; }
interface RequestSettlement { expectedVersion:Version; settlementId:Id; planRef:ProofRef; reason:string; }
interface SettlementView { settlementId:Id; returnId:Id; version:Version; settlementKey:Key; inputDigest:Digest|null; input:FinancePreparationInput|null; plan:FinancePlan|null; control:SettlementControl|null; result:FinanceResult|null; closure:FinanceClosure|SettlementLocalClosure|null; financeHead:OwnerHead; state:'RESOLVING'|'PLAN_READY'|'QUEUED'|'PARTIAL'|'APPLIED'|'NO_EFFECT_AUTHORIZED'|'FENCING'|'CLOSED'|'UNKNOWN'|'CONFLICT'; currentComplete:boolean; historicalPostedRefs:ProofRef[]; gates:Gate[]; }
interface SettlementPage { items:SettlementView[]; nextCursor:Key|null; coverage:Coverage; }
interface ResolutionObservation { ref:ProofRef; returnVersionRef:Ref; agreementRef:ProofRef; method:SettlementTerms['method']; scopeManifest:ProofRef; status:'PENDING'|'SATISFIED'|'DISPUTED'|'NOT_APPLICABLE'|'UNKNOWN'; cashReceiptRefs:ProofRef[]; offsetRefs:ProofRef[]; replacementSourceRefs:ProofRef[]; authority:ProofRef; currentHead:OwnerHead; }
```

本册新财务请求只基于真实已退范围；纯价差或先来信用为EXTERNAL_EXCEPTION保存，交原Owner，不造OUT。FinanceBasis必须覆盖原GRNI/AP/价值/付款及在途正向AP，采购不能按缺AP号猜无需调整；原Posted即使当前关联不齐仍保留。 [原文 L638](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:638>)、[原文 L640](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:640>)、[原文 L729](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:729>)

Finance拥有OriginalObligationKey：法人/账套、原凭证行及分配、原Source范围、币种/金额角色。MATCH更正、RETURN和手工调整全部入口共一个最终义务账，已完成＋在途＋未知都保护；两模块不同key争同12只能一个取得新额度。CREATE/REUSE_EXISTING/WAIT_EXISTING/NO_EFFECT_REQUIRED按准确范围、原因等价、原效果和专业证明区分，金额相等不等可复用。原价差credit2后退货原货款12，剩余由Finance裁决，不能再盲加12成14。 [原文 L681](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:681>)、[原文 L683](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:683>)、[原文 L685](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:685>)、[原文 L687](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:687>)、[原文 L689](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:689>)

SettlementScope先冻结本次完整已OUT行/part/range组和协议，再RESOLVING；Basis/Input/Plan未形成允许null，不造未来Ref。第一次execute后原Key/Input/Plan/执行epoch固定，UNKNOWN不得换键或金额。RequestSettlement最终核真实OUT/current SourceReceipt、专业Plan/权限/共享预算/协议，产生Control和Outbox；纠正已经OUT的原因不要求货重新在库或MRX CLEAR，也不开放原forward。 [原文 L675](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:675>)、[原文 L677](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:677>)、[原文 L695](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:695>)、[原文 L697](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:697>)、[原文 L699](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:699>)、[原文 L701](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:701>)

| 有权当前来源状态 | 准确动作与门 | 必须保留 | 出处 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| UNVALUED | Finance完整核GRNI/AP/价值/付款全部未确认义务后，返回完整NoEffectProof；采用原证明，无空execute | 原GR估价快照仍可存在，但它不是已Posted价值 | [原文 L721](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:721>) |
| GRNI_ONLY | Plan只列确实存在的GRNI/库存价值原义务及需调整腿；没有AP_CREDIT腿 | 原暂估/价值Posting和每个原币分项 | [原文 L722](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:722>) |
| AP_DRAFT | 枚举pendingForwardAPs，原MATCH先按既有更正/准确无效果关闭或专业有权草稿处置；未解决时canExecute=false | 原发票身份/草稿及范围，不能由RETURN删AP或造信用替代草稿关闭 | [原文 L723](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:723>) |
| AP_PENDING / PARTIALLY_POSTED | 原requestKey查全腿/当前头；MRX阻未来正向新效果，已Posted保留，未知暴露继续等待；精确处置证明后重取Basis/Plan | 每个已成功腿及仍可能发生的原路径；不能用部分结果推全部无效果 | [原文 L724](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:724>) |
| AP_POSTED | 对真实原AP义务及完整历史调整计算本次剩余额，Plan明确AP信用/抵扣及独立价值责任 | 原票据/税费/币种/原分配范围；不按最新PO价重估 | [原文 L725](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:725>) |
| PAID | Basis另列PAYMENT_ALLOCATION和真实付款/核销谱系，Finance给信用/抵扣计划；实际退款只读独立现金Owner结果 | 原付款不会被AP信用删除；现金未返仍PENDING | [原文 L726](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:726>) |
| MIXED / UNKNOWN | 按原义务分组但全范围manifest必须完整；任何未知影响本次范围的暴露则阻对应execute | 已知历史照常显示，不假0或强制全票成功 | [原文 L727](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:727>) |

无义务Plan只有准确全范围NoEffectProof才能ADOPT_NO_EFFECT，不发空execute；control/result可null。APPLIED须完整required legs各真实POSTED、Claim完成、准确input/plan/epoch/scope与当前head。价值调整和AP信用是不同账务责任，不能两次扣库存价值。协议现金退款必须真实cashReceiptRefs、抵扣须offsetRefs、替换须真实新来源；供应商邮件或信用完成不能显示到账。 [原文 L711](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:711>)、[原文 L713](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:713>)、[原文 L715](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:715>)

新增Stock /internal/wms/stock/return-purpose-views、Supplier logistics-destinations/read均是明确待真实采用的只读适配；有形状不代表已部署。MRX独立Control/transfer/final/physical/closure全部详见已落册MATCH第5/6节，返回UNKNOWN或NOT_OBSERVED均不可推无效果，Source不代MATCH写表。 [原文 L254](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:254>)、[原文 L262](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:262>)、[原文 L330](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:330>)、[原文 L417](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:417>)、[原文 L419](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:419>)

namespace H31 {

  receiptSliceRefs:Ref3[];rootMappingRef:Ref;poRevisionRef:Ref3;poLineId:Id;
  poSourcePortionRef:Ref3;sourceDomainRanges:STK.QuantityRange[];sourceUom:STK.VersionedCodeRef;
}

}

  poLineId:Id;poSourcePortionRef:Ref3;pieces:OriginMappingPiece[];
  coverage:'COMPLETE'|'UNKNOWN';mappingPolicyRef:Ref;observedAt:At;
}

export interface SourceGuardSeed {ref:Ref;authorizedDestinationRef:Ref;parentPermitRef:Ref;returnRef:Ref3;returnLineId:Id;executionPartKey:string;domainRef:Ref;approvedRanges:GR.ReceiptCoordinateRange[];epoch:N;controlRevision:N;domainGeneration:N;approvalRef:Ref;agreementRef:Ref;reverseImpactGuardRef:Ref;reverseImpactEpoch:N;coverage:'COMPLETE'|'UNKNOWN';adoptionRef:Ref;}
export interface GuardReadRequest {authorizationRef:Ref;permitBusinessDigest:H;}
export interface GuardRead {
  ref:Ref;authorizationRef:Ref;permitBusinessDigest:H;
  state:'HELD'|'STARTED'|'USED'|'CLOSING'|'CLOSED_NO_EFFECT'|'BLOCKED'|'UNKNOWN';
  epoch:N;controlRevision:N;domainGeneration:N;coverage:'COMPLETE'|'UNKNOWN';

  consumedRanges:GR.ReceiptCoordinateRange[];pendingRanges:GR.ReceiptCoordinateRange[];
  approvalRef:Ref;agreementRef:Ref;authorizedDestinationRef:Ref;reverseImpactGuardRef:Ref;reverseImpactEpoch:N;reverseImpactCoverage:'COMPLETE'|'UNKNOWN';sourceFenceRef:Ref|null;observedAt:At;
}
export interface ResourceKey {owner:string;resourceKey:string;stage:'CANONICAL'|'SCOPE'|'DETAIL';mode:'S'|'X';order:number;expectedRevision:string|null;}
export interface ResourcePlanRequest {requestRef:Ref;authorizationRef:Ref;permitBusinessDigest:H;manifestRef:Ref;stockPlanRef:STK.PlanRef;guardRef:Ref;phase:'START'|'EXECUTE';}
export interface ResourcePlan {ref:Ref;request:ResourcePlanRequest;resources:ResourceKey[];sourceCapacities:SourceCapacitySnapshot[];adoptionRef:Ref;planDigest:H;}
export interface ValidateInput {phase:'START'|'EXECUTE';requestRef:Ref;authorizationRef:Ref;permitBusinessDigest:H;manifestRef:Ref;stockPlanRef:STK.PlanRef;expectedGuardRef:Ref;resourcePlanRef:Ref;resourcePlanDigest:H;evaluatedAt:At;}
export interface ValidateResult {phase:'START'|'EXECUTE';requestRef:Ref;authorizationRef:Ref;permitBusinessDigest:H;manifestRef:Ref;stockPlanRef:STK.PlanRef;guardRef:Ref;resourcePlanRef:Ref;resourcePlanDigest:H;decision:'ALLOW_FOR_THIS_COMMIT'|'DENY'|'UNKNOWN';heldResources:ResourceKey[];heldUntilCommit:boolean;validationReceiptRef:Ref|null;reverseImpactGuardRef:Ref;reverseImpactEpoch:N;reverseImpactCoverage:'COMPLETE'|'UNKNOWN';reasons:string[];}
export interface StartRecordInput {requestRef:Ref;authorizationRef:Ref;permitBusinessDigest:H;validationReceiptRef:Ref;startedAt:At;}
export interface SourceStartReceipt {receipt:Receipt;requestRef:Ref;authorizationRef:Ref;permitBusinessDigest:H;state:'STARTED';currentGuard:GuardRead;}
export interface RecordPhysicalDispatchInput {requestRef:Ref;authorizationRef:Ref;permitBusinessDigest:H;validationReceiptRef:Ref;physicalFact:DispatchPhysicalFact;}
export interface SourceDispatchReceipt {sourceExecutionReceipt:Receipt;physicalFactRef:Ref;authorizationRef:Ref;permitBusinessDigest:H;stockReceipt:Receipt;recordedRanges:GR.ReceiptCoordinateRange[];domainGenerations:{domainRef:Ref;previous:N;current:N}[];remainingRanges:GR.ReceiptCoordinateRange[];}
export interface ReturnAuthorization {
  ref:Ref;returnRef:Ref3;returnLineId:Id;executionPartKey:string;
  permitRef:Ref;permitBusinessDigest:H;approvalRef:Ref;supplierAgreementRef:Ref;authorizedDestination:AuthorizedDestination;
  purposeRef:STK.VersionedCodeRef;origin:Origin;originMapping:OriginMapping;

}

export interface RequestRegistration { requestRef:Ref;requestDigest:H;workId:Id;state:'PREPARING'; }
export interface Preparation {
  requestRef:Ref;workRef:Ref;authorizationRef:Ref;permitBusinessDigest:H;
  stockPlan:STK.PostingPlanView;state:'READY'|'BLOCKED';blockingCodes:string[];
}
export interface StartRequest { workRef:Ref;expectedWorkRevision:N;authorizationRef:Ref;permitBusinessDigest:H;reason:string; }
export interface StartResult { workRef:Ref;state:'STARTED'|'BLOCKED'|'UNKNOWN';startReceipt:Receipt|null;currentSourceGuardRef:Ref|null;blockingCodes:string[]; }
export interface ExecuteRequest { workRef:Ref;expectedWorkRevision:N;authorizationRef:Ref;permitBusinessDigest:H;stockPlanRef:STK.PlanRef;startReceipt:Receipt;reason:string; }
export interface ResultLine { sourcePartKey:string;receiptLineId:Id;rootSpans:STK.QuantitySpan[];sourceDomainRanges:STK.QuantityRange[];quantity:Q;baseUom:STK.VersionedCodeRef;stockMovementRef:Receipt; }
export interface DispatchResult {
  resultRef:Ref;physicalFactRef:Ref;authorizedDestinationRef:Ref;workRef:Ref;authorizationRef:Ref;permitBusinessDigest:H;
  returnRef:Ref3;returnLineId:Id;executionPartKey:string;origin:Origin;
  lines:ResultLine[];stockOperation:STK.CommandResponse;sourceExecutionReceipt:Receipt;

}
export interface QueryRequest { requestKey:Id;requestDigest:H;authorizationRef:Ref;permitBusinessDigest:H; }
export type Outcome =
  {status:'PREPARING';registration:RequestRegistration;canInferNoEffect:false}|
  {status:'READY';preparation:Preparation;canInferNoEffect:false}|
  {status:'FOUND';result:DispatchResult;canInferNoEffect:false}|
  {status:'WAITING'|'UNKNOWN'|'BLOCKED'|'RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED';workRef:Ref|null;reasonCodes:string[];stockLookup:STK.OperationLookupResult|null;canInferNoEffect:false}|
  {status:'SEALED_NO_EFFECT'|'REJECTED_NO_EFFECT';closure:ClosureView;canInferNoEffect:true};
export interface CloseRequest { closeKey:Id;requestKey:Id;requestDigest:H;authorizationRef:Ref;permitBusinessDigest:H;reason:string; }
export interface DispatchFenceProof {

}

  dispatchFenceProofRef:Ref|null;reason:string;
}

  state:'FENCED_AWAITING_STOCK'|'FENCED_NEVER_DISPATCHED'|'EFFECT_ALREADY_RECORDED'|'UNKNOWN';
  sourceClosureAuthority:Ref|null;sourceFenceRef:Ref|null;registeredPaths:string[];
  coverage:'COMPLETE'|'UNKNOWN';budgetReleased:false;originalDispatchResultRef:Ref|null;
}
export interface SourceCloseFinalizeRequest {
  closeKey:Id;preparationRef:Ref;authorizationRef:Ref;permitBusinessDigest:H;

}

  outcome:'UNUSED_SCOPE_RELEASED'|'ACTUAL_SCOPE_RETAINED'|'UNKNOWN';
  releasedSourceRanges:STK.QuantityRange[];retainedSourceRanges:STK.QuantityRange[];
  sourceUom:STK.VersionedCodeRef;permanentFenceRemains:true;
}
export interface ClosureView {
  closureRef:Ref;requestKey:Id;requestDigest:H;authorizationRef:Ref;permitBusinessDigest:H;
  sourcePreparation:SourceClosePreparation|null;sourceFinalization:SourceCloseFinalizeResult|null;
  stockOperation:STK.CommandResponse|null;rejectedNoEffectProof:RejectedNoEffectProof|null;dispatchFenceProof:DispatchFenceProof|null;
  outcome:'SEALED_NO_EFFECT'|'REJECTED_NO_EFFECT'|'EFFECT_PRESENT'|'UNKNOWN';futureEffectsClosed:boolean;
  coverage:'COMPLETE'|'UNKNOWN';remainingUnresolvedParts:string[];
}
export interface LogisticsDestinationTarget {
  ref:Ref;supplierRef:Ref3;consigneeRef:Ref3;addressVersionRef:Ref;
  address:{countryCode:string;region:string;city:string;lines:string[];postalCode:string|null;};
  siteId:Id;purposeRef:STK.VersionedCodeRef;mode:'REAL'|'DEMO';
}
export interface AuthorizedDestination {
  ref:Ref;target:LogisticsDestinationTarget;returnRef:Ref3;returnLineId:Id;
  agreementRef:Ref;approvalRef:Ref;validFrom:At;validTo:At|null;mode:'REAL'|'DEMO';
}
export interface DispatchPhysicalFact {
  factRef:Ref;requestRef:Ref;startedWorkRef:Ref;authorizationRef:Ref;permitBusinessDigest:H;
  returnRef:Ref3;returnLineId:Id;executionPartKey:string;origin:Origin;
  manifestRef:Ref;stockIdentity:STK.ExecutionIdentity;stockOperation:STK.CommandResponse;
  lines:ResultLine[];authorizedDestinationRef:Ref;occurredAt:At;recordedAt:At;
  completeForPart:true;mode:'REAL'|'DEMO';
}
export interface PhysicalFactReadRequest {factRef:Ref;requestRef:Ref;authorizationRef:Ref;permitBusinessDigest:H;}
export type PhysicalFactReadResult = {status:'FOUND';fact:DispatchPhysicalFact;commitKnowledge:'COMMITTED'}|{status:'NOT_OBSERVED'|'UNAVAILABLE';factRef:Ref;commitKnowledge:'UNKNOWN';canInferNoEffect:false;};
export interface RejectedNoEffectInput {
  stockIdentity:STK.ExecutionIdentity;stockIntentRef:STK.PlanRef;sourceExecutionRef:Ref;
  manifestRef:Ref|null;actionDefinitionRef:Ref|null;sourceFenceRef:Ref;
  lookupRequest:STK.OperationLookupRequest;lookupResult:STK.OperationLookupResult;
}
export interface RejectedNoEffectProof {
  ref:Ref;input:RejectedNoEffectInput;registeredPaths:string[];
  canonicalAndAllLeavesMatched:true;allAliasesPermanentlyClosed:true;
  actualOrReconciliationEffectsAbsent:true;coverage:'COMPLETE';observedAt:At;
}
export interface SourceCapacityMetric {
 metricRef:STK.VersionedCodeRef;capacityUomRef:STK.VersionedCodeRef;conversionRef:Ref;
 capacityDomainKey:string;capacityHeadRef:Ref;capacityRevision:N;
 confirmedBase:STK.TotalDecimal;unreceivedBase:STK.TotalDecimal;plannedPhysicalDelta:string;
 limit:STK.TotalDecimal;ruleRef:Ref;mappingHeadRef:Ref;occupancyRef:Ref;
}
export interface SourceCapacitySnapshot {
 ref:Ref;schema:'H31-SOURCE-CAPACITY-2';stockPlanRef:STK.PlanRef;
 sourceLocation:{warehouseId:Id;locationId:Id;};sourceLineKeys:string[];rootSpans:STK.QuantitySpan[];
 metricSetRef:Ref;metricSetRevision:N;metrics:SourceCapacityMetric[];coverage:'COMPLETE';
 applicability:{kind:'CONTROLLED';}|{kind:'NO_REGISTERED_METRICS';determinationRef:Ref;reasonCode:'CURRENT_METRIC_SET_EMPTY';};
 unreceivedEffect:'NONE_EXISTING_PHYSICAL_REDUCTION';observedAt:At;
}
export interface SourceCapacityResult {
 stockReceipt:Receipt;sourceSnapshotRef:Ref;
 metrics:{capacityDomainKey:string;previousRevision:N;currentRevision:N;confirmedBefore:STK.TotalDecimal;confirmedAfter:STK.TotalDecimal;unreceivedBefore:STK.TotalDecimal;unreceivedAfter:STK.TotalDecimal;capacityUomRef:STK.VersionedCodeRef;}[];
}

}

```

OUT前缀/internal/wms/out/v1/purchase-return-dispatches提供prepare/start/execute/query/close；Source MainUow是实际同DbConnection/DbTransaction注入对象，不是JSON字段/token。Validate ALLOW_FOR_THIS_COMMIT只在真实锁持至本次commit才heldUntilCommit=true，UNKNOWN/DENY receipt=null。原QuantityReceiptRef不是凭movementId猜版本1；Movement真实ID须从同Stock response另核。 [原文 L54245](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54245>)、[原文 L54247](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54247>)、[原文 L54309](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54309>)

对PO RETURN唯一物理Producer首批为注册WMS_RETURN代理OUT；RETURN只观察/唤醒原链。Finance.AP独占PO INVOICE_CREDIT。若以后采用另一独占转换Producer必须明确排他，不能两边计量。GR ReturnNotice用PUR_RETURN_OBSERVER逐实际effect流：父Return/Line/Part/SourcePart/原Movement组成稳定identity，父商业版不当物理消息版。真实4/6是两流各v1，v2=3替换原4而不追加3；Stock纠正后10→8→0为同流全快照，原GR Received/E不变。 [原文 L530](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:530>)、[原文 L532](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:532>)、[原文 L542](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:542>)、[原文 L550](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:550>)、[原文 L552](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:552>)、[原文 L554](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:554>)、[原文 L556](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:556>)、[原文 L558](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:558>)

namespace OUT {
export interface ShipmentEffectIdentity {kind:Kind;originalResultRef:Ref;originalLineKey:string;originalStockMovementId:Id;originalStockReceipt:Receipt;}

  originalPlan:STK.PostingPlanView;sourceClosureRef:Ref;
  appliedRanges:{originalLineKey:string;rootSpans:Span[];}[];
  reverseMovementId:Id;observedAt:At;coverage:'COMPLETE';

  originalRootSpans:Span[];reversedRootSpans:Span[];effectiveRootSpans:Span[];
  originalQuantity:Q;effectiveQuantity:Total;baseUom:Uom;
  sourceRanges:Range[];sourceUom:Uom;sourceMappingRef:Ref;

export interface CorrectionLinkResult {associationRef:Ref;originalResultRef:Ref;correctionEvidenceRef:Ref;state:'APPLIED'|'WAITING_STOCK'|'WAITING_PREDECESSOR'|'CONFLICT';snapshots:ShipmentEffectiveSnapshot[];receipt:Receipt|null;quantityEffect:false;reasons:string[];}

 {status:'FOUND';snapshot:ShipmentEffectiveSnapshot;currentHeadRef:Ref;coverage:'COMPLETE';sourceObservation:STKCD.DiscoverySnapshot;appliedSourceSetDigest:H;effectiveAsOf:At;}|
 {status:'HISTORY_FOUND';snapshot:ShipmentEffectiveSnapshot;currentCompletenessClaimed:false;}|
 {status:'UNKNOWN';identity:ShipmentEffectIdentity;lastConfirmedRef:Ref|null;sourceObservation:STKCD.DiscoverySnapshot|null;pendingCorrectionRefs:Ref[];refreshRef:Ref|null;reasonCodes:string[];canInferZero:false;};
export interface EffectiveRefreshInput {requestKey:Id;identity:ShipmentEffectIdentity;sourceSnapshotRef:Ref;reason:string;}
export interface EffectiveRefreshResult {refreshRef:Ref;identity:ShipmentEffectIdentity;sourceSnapshotRef:Ref;state:'WAITING_SOURCE'|'WAITING_CORRECTIONS'|'APPLIED_AS_OF'|'CONFLICT';appliedSourceSetDigest:H|null;snapshotRef:Ref|null;remainingCorrectionRefs:Ref[];receipt:Receipt|null;quantityEffect:false;}

export interface EffectiveSnapshotEvent {eventId:Id;producer:'WMS_OUT';schema:'OUT-EFFECTIVE-SNAPSHOT-1';businessRef:Ref;identity:ShipmentEffectIdentity;revision:Rev;previousRef:Ref|null;snapshot:ShipmentEffectiveSnapshot;semanticDigest:H;occurredAt:At;mode:'REAL'|'DEMO';}

}
```

OUT有效量只解释对该原Movement的准确错误逆向，不把新一批或正常商业退货当负发运；E0减完整无重叠已证逆向范围，经原Mapping回原Source坐标。关联按StockCorrectionCanonical全组唯一，一次更正跨多个结果须完整batch原子CAS所有heads，缺一行WAITING，不能重做Stock。消费者原事实和snapshot v1归同effect，当前快照不新增量；缺v2先见v3保存高头并失效，OCX03补OUT已成版本。 [原文 L90359](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:90359>)、[原文 L90365](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:90365>)、[原文 L90375](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:90375>)、[原文 L90377](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:90377>)、[原文 L90381](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:90381>)、[原文 L90383](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:90383>)、[原文 L90389](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:90389>)、[原文 L90391](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:90391>)

父Case关闭先封全生命周期新Part/版本/别名并捕完整Slot，包括未生成Authorization/child注册。pre-Authorization只能全调用路径未产生可执行授权且永久封住才本地释放，不能伪Stock Closure。已授权先FENCE_ONLY释放0；never-sent有真实排他证明才BIND_NO_DISPATCH；可能发出但无Plan仍UNKNOWN查原metadata，查回才同closeKey BIND_STOCK。Stock原决定点POSTED优先，有SEALED或准确永久REJECTED全叶证明才无效果。 [原文 L518](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:518>)、[原文 L520](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:520>)、[原文 L522](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:522>)、[原文 L524](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:524>)、[原文 L54263](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54263>)、[原文 L54265](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54265>)、[原文 L54267](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54267>)、[原文 L54268](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54268>)、[原文 L54269](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54269>)、[原文 L54270](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54270>)

REJECTED_NO_EFFECT消费Stock原永久REJECTED＋NO_NEW_EFFECT，不强改SEALED、不造Movement/Closure；HTTP409或单个状态字段不足。Source最终核完整Canonical/Intent/所有叶/别名及无实际/认账，未全齐UNKNOWN释放0。Part未用关闭仅返自己父许可，另一Case仍冲突；只有父永久fence与全槽SourceClose、MRX及保护继承同commit才返全局。旧sealed Part永不复活，真实新动作需有据新Part/批准。 [原文 L524](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:524>)、[原文 L526](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:526>)、[原文 L562](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:562>)、[原文 L564](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:564>)、[原文 L54348](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54348>)、[原文 L54350](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54350>)、[原文 L54352](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:54352>)

Finance取消无execute且prepare采用确实零效果，可本地锁中全路径fence/计数0形成LocalClosure，Plan可null；有STARTED/未知/真实效果必须查原Plan再Finance全腿关闭。SettlementControl FENCE只controlRevision+1，第一次execute的epoch保持1，Closure必须覆盖原执行代及全部别名，不能改epoch2后查不到就当无效果。EFFECT_EXISTS保历史，不释放实退/MRX；父无物理但有外来财务事实也不能WITHDRAWN_NO_EFFECT。 [原文 L703](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:703>)、[原文 L752](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:752>)、[原文 L758](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:758>)、[原文 L848](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:848>)

### 持久query generation、attempt、workerEpoch和Head/Case CAS全部类型

[原文 L602](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:602>)

```ts
interface PhysicalQueryToken { queryId:Id; generation:Seq; attempt:Seq; workerEpoch:Seq; }
interface PhysicalCurrentQueryStart { commandId:Id; returnId:Id; returnLineId:Id; effectIdentity:OUT.ShipmentEffectIdentity; reason:'USER_REFRESH'|'RECONCILE'|'RECOVER_SOURCE'|'NEW_EFFECT_EVENT'; }
interface PhysicalCurrentQueryResume { commandId:Id; queryId:Id; expectedWorkerEpoch:Seq; reason:'RESTART'|'TIMEOUT_RETRY'; }
interface PhysicalCurrentQueryWork { queryId:Id; returnId:Id; returnLineId:Id; effectIdentity:OUT.ShipmentEffectIdentity; commandId:Id; generation:Seq; activeToken:PhysicalQueryToken; request:OUT.EffectiveReadRequest; state:'PENDING'|'APPLIED'|'UNKNOWN'|'FAILED'|'SUPERSEDED'; registeredAt:At; deadline:At; errorCodes:Key[]; }
interface PhysicalSourceFloor { sourceSnapshotRef:EvidenceRef; stockCutoffVector:{poolId:Id;sequence:Seq;}[]; sourceSetDigest:Digest; }
interface PhysicalCurrentKnowledgeHead { returnId:Id; returnLineId:Id; effectIdentity:OUT.ShipmentEffectIdentity; generation:Counter; rowVersion:Version; queryId:Id|null; activeToken:PhysicalQueryToken|null; state:'UNOBSERVED'|'PENDING'|'CURRENT_AT_AS_OF'|'UNKNOWN'|'CONFLICT'; currentKnowledgeRef:ProofRef|null; lastConfirmedKnowledgeRef:ProofRef|null; lastConfirmedQuantity:Q8|null; currentQuantity:Q8|null; verifiedAsOf:At|null; observedSourceFrontier:PhysicalSourceFloor[]; observedEffectiveRevision:Counter; reasonCodes:Key[]; }
interface PhysicalCompletionProjection { returnId:Id; revision:Version; headVector:{effectIdentity:OUT.ShipmentEffectIdentity;generation:Counter;rowVersion:Version;state:PhysicalCurrentKnowledgeHead['state'];}[]; physicalGate:boolean; otherGatesCurrent:boolean; currentCompletion:boolean; verifiedAsOf:At|null; }
interface PhysicalCurrentQueryView { work:PhysicalCurrentQueryWork; head:PhysicalCurrentKnowledgeHead; caseProjection:PhysicalCompletionProjection; }
type PhysicalCurrentQueryOutcome={kind:'OWNER_RESPONSE';response:OUT.EffectiveReadResult;}|{kind:'FAILED';errorCodes:Key[];}|{kind:'TIMED_OUT';errorCodes:Key[];};
interface PhysicalCurrentQueryReply { token:PhysicalQueryToken; completedAt:At; outcome:PhysicalCurrentQueryOutcome; }
interface PhysicalCurrentApplyReceipt { ref:ProofRef; token:PhysicalQueryToken; responseDigest:Digest; knowledgeRef:ProofRef|null; disposition:'CURRENT_APPLIED'|'CURRENT_INVALIDATED'|'HISTORY_ONLY'|'CONFLICT'; reasonCodes:Key[]; beforeHeadVersion:Version; head:PhysicalCurrentKnowledgeHead; caseProjection:PhysicalCompletionProjection; quantityEffect:false; }
```

DATA33原响应知识不是当前指针；唯一DATA34按环境/租户/Case/Line/完整EffectIdentity保存CurrentHead。T15新command分配generation+1及Work/attempt1/workerEpoch1，旧Work superseded；发网络前Head=PENDING/currentQuantity=null、Case完成false同commit，保lastConfirmed与来源frontier。相同command只回原Work/当前Head，不再分配。 [原文 L600](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:600>)、[原文 L616](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:616>)、[原文 L618](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:618>)

T16先按queryId/generation/attempt/epoch/outcome.kind保存不可变Reply；同类同token异文冲突，网络结果与timeout为不同类。只有完整token同时等Head与Work且Work PENDING，再对重读的Head.rowVersion和CaseProjectionRevision CAS才可当前应用。旧token通常HISTORY_ONLY，不改Head/Case任何位；若首次揭示更高source cut/更高真实revision仍必须推进观察下界并使旧当前失效，不能一概丢旧响应。 [原文 L620](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:620>)、[原文 L624](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:624>)、[原文 L630](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:630>)

活动FOUND还须完整sourceSet覆盖、连续snapshot不低ObservedEffectiveRevision、cut支配全部完整Pool来源frontier；来源向量不可比保全部极大下界并UNKNOWN，不拼cut、不按HTTP时间排序。活动UNKNOWN/HISTORY_FOUND/失败/超时都currentQuantity=null/Case false，保历史。物理当前8不自动改原信用12或让Finance完成；Head与全Case门必须一个事务，不能头未知但Case仍绿色。 [原文 L622](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:622>)、[原文 L624](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:624>)

T17仅最新generation且可恢复/租约确过期的Work CAS epoch+1、attempt+1，先Head PENDING/Case false与OwnerCall再发全新current读；旧线程、旧超时不能覆盖新token或已APPLIED终态。已成功Work要新当前值需T15新command；重放ApplyReceipt是历史回执，前端不得拿里面旧Head覆盖当前GET。T11 rebuild固定全部Head向量、frontier、CaseRevision，有任何新查询/事件/响应变化即弃候选。 [原文 L626](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:626>)、[原文 L628](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:628>)、[原文 L630](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:630>)、[原文 L632](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:632>)、[原文 L634](<D:/CP6/docs/CP6_完整成果归档_20261009/完整成果/docs/originals/1f/1f904a10280b2cbb__PUR-RETURN-01_采购退货与供应商结算调整_前后端开发Spec_v1.0.3_REVIEW_R3_CANDIDATE.md:634>)
